Form Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : AK2010 / Uygulama : PFRM7S
Makro Kodu : ALTIN-1
Hangi Tablo için : STOK70
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : 
Açýklama : ALTIN KABLO SATIÞ FATURASI - NET FÝYAT
Kod : 
Public sayfaBoyu, sayfaEni, sonSatir, satirSonu, TutarOndalik, ToplamTutar, S70T
Public UstMargin, SolMargin, SagMargin, AltMargin, satiryuksekligi, cpi
Public siraNo, satirBaslangic, SatirBitis, solTutar, GenTutar, Grid

Sub PrintForm()
	STOK70T.SafePack()
	Doc.CreateTableObject "drr=STOK70T", "TMPCOPY"
	Set S70T = Doc.GetTableObject("drr")
	Set S70T1 = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK70T")
	S70T.AppendTable(S70T1)
	For i = 1 to S70T.GetRowCount()
		if trim(S70T.KOD(i)) >= "E101" and trim(S70T.KOD(i)) <= "E103" Then
			S70T.KOD(i) = Doc.Kod_SeviyeAl(S70T.KOD(i), -1)
			S70T.LOTNUMBER(i) = ""
			S70T.SERINO(i) = ""
		End if
		S70T.SEVKPAKETLOTNO(i) = "F" & S70T.TL_FIYAT(i) * (100-S70T.YISKONTO_1(i)) / 100
	Next
	S70T.GroupBy2("KOD+SEVKPAKETLOTNO")

	Dim pe1
	sayfaBoyu = 11 * 25.4
	sayfaEni = 8.5 * 25.4
	
	ToplamTutar = 0
	siraNo = 0
	satiryuksekligi = 4.3
	UstMargin = satirYuksekligi 
	AltMargin = SayfaBoyu - 10 
	SolMargin = 8
	SagMargin = SayfaEni - 8
	satirBaslangic = UstMargin + 12 * satiryuksekligi
	satirSonu = satirBaslangic + 23 * satirYuksekligi
	cpi = 17

	'if STOK70E.DO_ISKONTOX_ORANI <> 74 Then msgbox "1"
	Set pe1 = doc.CreatePrintEngineOutput()
	res = pe1.Open(0, "Fatura-" & trim(STOK70E.EVRAKNO), SayfaBoyu,SayfaEni,"")

	res = pe1.SetConsolePrintOpt(1)
	res = pe1.ConsoleOutput_SetLinesPerPage_P(66)
	res = pe1.ConsoleOutput_SetCharsPerInch(cpi)

	Call Alanlar()
	satirSayisi = S70T.GetRecCount()
	Call Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
	For i = 1 To satirSayisi
		Call Print_form_satir(pe1, i)
	Next
	Call Print_form_sayfasonu(pe1)

	res = pe1.Show()
	res = Doc.ActivateAfterMacro(1)
End Sub

Sub Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
	res = pe1.selectfont("Courier New", 7)
	res = pe1.setbold(0)

	CHKodu = STOK70E.CH_KODU
	CHAdi = Doc.GetDBFieldvalue("CARI00", "AD", CHKodu,"")
	Adres1 = Doc.GetDBFieldvalue("CARI00", "ADRES_1", CHKodu,"")
	Adres2 = Doc.GetDBFieldvalue("CARI00", "ADRES_2", CHKodu,"")
	Adres3 = Doc.GetDBFieldvalue("CARI00", "ADRES_3", CHKodu,"")
	VergiDairesi = Doc.GetDBFieldvalue("CARI00", "VERGIDAIRESI", CHKodu,"")
	VergiNo = Doc.GetDBFieldvalue("CARI00", "VERGINO", CHKodu,"")
	Tarih = Doc.strtonordate(STOK70E.TARIH)
	EvrakNo = STOK70E.EVRAKNO
	
	res = pe1.write(ustmargin + satirYuksekligi, solmargin + 1,satiryuksekligi, 80, trim(CHAdi))
	res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi, pe1.GetLeft, satiryuksekligi, 80, trim(Adres1))
	res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi, pe1.GetLeft, satiryuksekligi, 80, trim(Adres2))
	res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi, pe1.GetLeft, satiryuksekligi, 80, trim(Adres3))
	res = pe1.write(UstMargin + 7 * satirYuksekligi, solmargin + 35,satiryuksekligi, 110, trim(VergiDairesi) & " / " & trim(VergiNo))
	yVergiNo = pe1.GetTop

	res = pe1.write(ustmargin + satirYuksekligi, solmargin+160, satiryuksekligi, 35, "BÜYÜK MÜKELLEFLER V.D.")
	res = pe1.write(ustmargin + 2 * satirYuksekligi, solmargin+165, satiryuksekligi, 30, Tarih)
	res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop + 2 * satirYuksekligi, pe1.GetLeft, satiryuksekligi, 30, trim(CHKodu))
	
	if pe1.GetPagecount > 1 Then
		res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
		res = pe1.Write(satirBaslangic, solTutar - 20, 5, 20, "NAKLÝ YEKÜN")
		res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
		res = pe1.Write(satirBaslangic, solTutar, satiryuksekligi, GenTutar - 2, Doc.NumFormat(ToplamTutar, 20, TutarOndalik))
		res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	End if
End Sub
Sub Print_form_satir(pe1, satirNo)
	AlanDegeri = ""
	sonSatir = pe1.GetBottom()
	If sonSatir >= satirSonu Then
		Call Print_form_Arasayfasonu(pe1)
		siraNo = 1
		res = pe1.PageBreak()
		Call Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
	End If		
	siraNo = siraNo + 1
	satirpozr = SatirBaslangic + (siraNo - 1) * satiryuksekligi

	res = pe1.setbold(0)
	Sol = SolMargin + 1
	res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")
	ToplamTutar = ToplamTutar + S70T.TL_ISKONTOLU_TUTAR(satirNo)
	For i = 0 To UBound(Grid,1)
		SutunGenisligi = Grid(i,1) 
		AlanAdi = Grid(i,0)
		
		If AlanAdi = "_MKOD" Then
			StokKodu = S70T.KOD(satirNo)
			CHKodu = STOK70E.CH_KODU
			MusteriStokKodu = Doc.GetChStokKodu(StokKodu,CHKodu)
			AlanDegeri = "(" & trim(MusteriStokKodu) & ")"
		ElseIf AlanAdi = "_IFIYAT" Then
			AlanDegeri = Doc.CDbl(Mid(S70T.SEVKPAKETLOTNO(satirNo),2))
		ElseIf AlanAdi = "_KODAD" Then
			StokKodu = S70T.KOD(satirNo)
			if Left(StokKodu,2) = "ZZ" then
				Aciklama = S70T.NOTES(satirNo)
				if trim(Aciklama) <> "" then
					AlanDegeri = Aciklama
				else
					AlanDegeri = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00","AD",StokKodu,"")
				end if
			else
				AlanDegeri = trim(S70T.KOD(satirNo)) & " - " & trim(Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00","AD",StokKodu,""))
			end if
		Else
			AlanDegeri = S70T.GetFieldValue(AlanAdi,SatirNo)
			if AlanAdi = "TL_ISKONTOLU_TUTAR" Then
				solTutar = sol
				GenTutar = SutunGenisligi
			end if
		End If			
		res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
		If Grid(i,4) = "N" Then
			res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
			Ondalik = Grid(i,6)
			AlanDegeri = Doc.NumFormat(AlanDegeri,18,Ondalik)
'***
		If AlanAdi = "SF_MIKTAR" Then
				AlanDegeri = Doc.NumFormat(AlanDegeri,18,0)

			end if
'***

		ElseIf Grid(i,2) = "birim" Then
			res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
			AlanDegeri = Trim(AlanDegeri)
		ElseIf Grid(i,4) = "D" Then
			AlanDegeri = Doc.StrtoNordate(AlanDegeri)
		End If			
		res = pe1.write(satirpozr, Sol, satiryuksekligi, SutunGenisligi - 2, AlanDegeri)
		
		Sol = Sol + SutunGenisligi						
	Next					 
	sonSatir = pe1.GetBottom()
End Sub
Sub Print_form_Arasayfasonu(pe1)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.Write(satirSonu, solTutar - 20, satiryuksekligi, 20, "ARA TOPLAM")
	res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
	res = pe1.Write(satirSonu, solTutar, satiryuksekligi, GenTutar - 2, Doc.NumFormat(ToplamTutar, 20, TutarOndalik))
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
End Sub
Sub Print_form_sayfasonu(pe1)
	Toplam = STOK70E.TL_NEWTOPLAM
	NetToplam = STOK70E.TL_ISKONTOLUTOPLAM
	Toplam = NetToplam
	KDVTutari = STOK70E.TL_KDVLERTOPLAM
	BeyanKDV = KDVTutari - STOK70E.TL_KESINTI1
	Nettoplam = STOK70E.TL_ISKONTOLUTOPLAM
	kdvtoplam = STOK70E.TL_KDVLERTOPLAM
	kdvoran = Doc.Div(kdvtoplam, Nettoplam) * 100
	KDVDahilTutar = STOK70E.TL_KDVDAHILTOPLAM
	GenelToplam = KDVDahilTutar + STOK70E.TL_KESINTI1
	GenelToplam1 = STOK70E.TL_KDVDAHILTOPLAM
	IskontoToplam = STOK70E.TL_ISKONTOLARTOPLAMI
	IskontoToplam = 0
	DipIskontoOrani = STOK70E.DO_ISKONTOX_ORANI
	DipIskontoOrani = 0

	Ondalik = TutarOndalik
	Toplam = Doc.NumFormat(Toplam,20,Ondalik)	
	Iskontotoplam = Doc.NumFormat(iskontoToplam,20,Ondalik)
	NetToplam = Doc.NumFormat(NetToplam,20,Ondalik)
	KDVTutari = Doc.NumFormat(KDVTutari,20,Ondalik)
	GenelToplam = Doc.NumFormat(GenelToplam,20,Ondalik)
	KDVOndalik = Doc.SigDig(kdvoran)
	if KDVOndalik > 2 Then KDVOndalik = 2
	kdvoran = Doc.Round(kdvoran, 1)
	KDVOndalik = Doc.SigDig(kdvoran)
	kdvoran = Doc.NumFormat(kdvoran,10,KDVOndalik)
	if DipIskontoOrani > 0 Then
		IskontoOndalik = Doc.SigDig(DipIskontoOrani)
		if IskontoOndalik > 1 Then IskontoOndalik = 1
		DipIskontoOrani = Doc.NumFormat(DipIskontoOrani,10,IskontoOndalik)	
		IskontoStr = "% " & DipIskontoOrani
	End if
	if STOK70E.TL_KESINTI1 > 0 Then
		Gen11 = 50
		res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
		res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi, solTutar - Gen11, satiryuksekligi, Gen11, "Mal Bedeli")
		res = pe1.Setalign("RIGHT")
		res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop, solTutar, satiryuksekligi, GenTutar - 2, Toplam)
		res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
		res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi, solTutar - Gen11, satiryuksekligi, Gen11, "KDV (%" & KdvOran & ")")
		res = pe1.Setalign("RIGHT")
		res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop, solTutar, satiryuksekligi, GenTutar - 2, KDVTutari)
		res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
		res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi, solTutar - Gen11, satiryuksekligi, Gen11, "Toplam")
		res = pe1.Setalign("RIGHT")
		res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop, solTutar, satiryuksekligi, GenTutar - 2, GenelToplam)
		res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
		res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi, solTutar - Gen11, satiryuksekligi, Gen11, "(-) Tevkif edilecek KDV (%" & STOK70E.TEVKIFAT_ORANI & ")")
		res = pe1.Setalign("RIGHT")
		res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop, solTutar, satiryuksekligi, GenTutar - 2, Doc.NumFormat(STOK70E.TL_KESINTI1, 20 ,2))
		res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
		res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi, solTutar - Gen11, satiryuksekligi, Gen11, "Beyan Edilecek KDV(%" & 100 - STOK70E.TEVKIFAT_ORANI & ")")
		res = pe1.Setalign("RIGHT")
		res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop, solTutar, satiryuksekligi, GenTutar - 2, Doc.NumFormat(BeyanKDV,20,2))
		res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
		res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi, solTutar - Gen11, satiryuksekligi, Gen11, "Tahsil Edilecek Toplam Tutar")
		res = pe1.Setalign("RIGHT")
		res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop, solTutar, satiryuksekligi, GenTutar - 2, Doc.NumFormat(KDVDahilTutar, 20 ,2))
	Else
		DipToplamBasla = UstMargin + 36 * satirYuksekligi
		res = pe1.Setalign("RIGHT")
 		res = pe1.write(DipToplamBasla, solTutar, satiryuksekligi, GenTutar-2, Toplam)
		res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
		res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop + 2 * satirYuksekligi, solTutar - 15, satiryuksekligi, 15, IskontoStr)
		res = pe1.Setalign("RIGHT")
		res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop, solTutar, 5, GenTutar - 2, iskontoToplam)
		res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop + 2 * satirYuksekligi, solTutar, satiryuksekligi, genTutar - 2, NetToplam)
		res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
		res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop + 2 * satirYuksekligi, solTutar - 15, satiryuksekligi, 15, "% " & trim(kdvoran))
		res = pe1.Setalign("RIGHT")
		res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop, solTutar, 5, GenTutar - 2, KDVTutari)
		res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop + 2 * satirYuksekligi, solTutar, 5, GenTutar - 2, GenelToplam)
	End if

	res = pe1.Setalign("LEFT")
 	res = pe1.write(UstMargin + 38 * satirYuksekligi, solMargin + 100, satiryuksekligi, 30, Doc.StrToNorDate(STOK70E.VALORTARIH))
	sol = SolMargin + 20
'	If trim(nitelik) = "NC" Then
'		res = pe1.write(dipsatirpozr - 4 * satirYuksekligi, sol, 5, 130, "Ýþ bu Fatura Muhteviyatý mallar Numune olarak sevkedilmiþtir.")
'		res = pe1.write(dipsatirpozr - 3 * satirYuksekligi, sol, 5, 130, "Bedeli Tahsil edilmeyecektir")
'	End If

	res = pe1.Setalign("LEFT")
	Yalniz = Doc.NumToText_TL(KDVDahilTutar)
	res = pe1.write(UstMargin + 50 * satirYuksekligi, solmargin + 15, satiryuksekligi, 130, Yalniz)
	res = pe1.Setbold(0)

	IrsaliyeUst = UstMargin + 42 * satirYuksekligi
	Set S70 = Doc.Select2("dde333", "STOK70T", "", "", -1)	
	S70.GroupBy "IRSALIYENO"
	For i = 1 To S70.GetRecCount()
		IrsaliyeNo = S70.IRSALIYENO(i)
		IrsaliyeTarihi = doc.GetDbFieldValue("STOK60E", "TARIH", irsaliyeno, "")
		if trim(IrsaliyeNo) <> "" Then
			sira = sira + 1
			res = pe1.write(IrsaliyeUst + (sira - 1) * satirYuksekligi, solMargin + 10,satiryuksekligi, 40, Doc.StrToNorDate(IrsaliyeTarihi))
			res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop, solmargin + 40,satiryuksekligi, 40, IrsaliyeNo)
		End if
	Next
End Sub

Sub Alanlar()
	ReDim Grid(6,6)

	Grid(0,0) = "_KODAD"
	Grid(1,0) = "SF_MIKTAR"
	Grid(2,0) = "SF_SF_UNIT"
	Grid(3,0) = ""
	Grid(4,0) = "_IFIYAT"
	Grid(5,0) = "TL_ISKONTOLU_TUTAR"

	Grid(0,1) = 90
	Grid(1,1) = 23
	Grid(2,1) = 15
	Grid(3,1) = 12
	Grid(4,1) = 21
	Grid(5,1) = 25
	
	Grid(0,4) = "C"
	Grid(1,4) = "N"
	Grid(2,4) = "C"
	Grid(3,4) = "N"
	Grid(4,4) = "N"
	Grid(5,4) = "N"
	
	For i = 0 To UBound(Grid,1) 
		AlanAdi = Grid(i,0)
		If Grid(i,4) = "N" Then
			ondalik = S70T.MaxSigDig(AlanAdi)	
			if AlanAdi = "_IFIYAT" Then Ondalik = 4
			if AlanAdi = "TL_ISKONTOLU_TUTAR" Then
				Ondalik = 2
				TutarOndalik = Ondalik
			End if
			Grid(i,6) = Ondalik		
		End If
	Next	
	For i = 0 To uBound(Grid,1) - 1
		ToplamEn = ToplamEn + Grid(i,1)
	Next
End Sub
Sub CizgiCiz(pe1, Ust, sol, Boy, En, Karakter)
	KarakterSayisi = Doc.Floor(En  * cpi / 25.4)
	res = pe1.Write(Ust, sol, Boy, En, String(KarakterSayisi,Karakter))
End Sub                                         


----------------------------------------------------------------
Form Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : AK2010 / Uygulama : PFRM7S
Makro Kodu : BANKA
Hangi Tablo için : CARI20GD
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : CARI20BV
Açýklama : Banka Ýþlem Formu
Kod : 
Public sayfaBoyu, sayfaEni, UstMargin, SolMargin, SagMargin, AltMargin, satirYuksekligi
Sub PrintForm()
	CARI20T.SafePack()
	Set ss = Doc.CreateSObject("BATCHERRORMODE")
	sayfaBoyu = 297
	sayfaEni = 210

	UstMargin = 30
	AltMargin = SayfaBoyu - 30
	SolMargin = 10
	SagMargin = SayfaEni - 13
	satiryuksekligi = 4
	
	Set pe1 = Doc.CreatePrintEngineOutput()
	res = pe1.Open(0, "Banka Islem Formu-" & trim(CARI20E.EVRAKNO), SayfaBoyu, SayfaEni, "")

	satirSayisi = CARI20T.GetRecCount()
	For i = 1 To satirSayisi
		if i > 1 Then pe1.PageBreak
		Call Print_form1(pe1, i)
	Next
	res = pe1.Show()
	res = Doc.ActivateAfterMacro(1)
End Sub

Sub Print_form1(pe1, satirNo)
	res = pe1.nopen()
        
	res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 13)
	res = pe1.setalign("CENTER")
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(UstMargin,SolMargin,10, sagMargin - solMargin,"Banka Ýþlem Formu")

	EvrakNo = CARI20E.EVRAKNO
	Tarih = Doc.StrtoNordate(CARI20E.TARIH)
	BankaCHKodu = CARI20T.KOD(satirNo)
	Set B00C = Doc.Select1("dsdee333", "BNK00C", "KOD='" & BankaCHKodu & "'", "", -1)
	if B00C.GetRowCount = 0 Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox trim(BankaCHKodu) & " banka cari hesap kartýnda Banka Hesaplarý sayfasý doldurulmamýþ", "", MB_OK
		Exit Sub
	End if

	AliciCHKodu = CARI20T.KARSI_CODE(satirNo)
	Set B00C1 = Doc.Select1("dsdee333SS", "BNK00C", "KOD='" & AliciCHKodu & "'", "", -1)
	if B00C1.GetRowCount = 0 Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox trim(AliciCHKodu) & " cari hesap kartýnda Banka Hesaplarý sayfasý doldurulmamýþ", "", MB_OK
		Exit Sub
	End if

	BankaAdi = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("BNK00","AD", B00C.BANKA_KODU,"") 
	SubeAdi = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("BNK00T","SUBE_ADI", B00C.BANKA_KODU & B00C.SUBE_KODU,"KOD+SUBE_KODU")
	HesapNo = B00C.HESAPNO
	Adres1 = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("CARI00","ADRES_1",BankaCHKodu,"") 
	Adres2 = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("CARI00","ADRES_2",BankaCHKodu,"") 
	Adres3 = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("CARI00","ADRES_3",BankaCHKodu,"") 
	Tel = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("CARI00","TELEFONNO_1",BankaCHKodu,"") 
	Faks = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("CARI00","FAXNO",BankaCHKodu,"") 
	VergiDairesi = "Büyük Mükellefler V.D."
	VergiNo = "0610025337"
	email = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("CARI00","EMAIL",BankaCHKodu,"")

	AliciCHAdi = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("CARI00","AD",AliciCHKodu,"")
	AliciBankaAdi = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("BNK00","AD", B00C1.BANKA_KODU,"") 
	AliciSubeAdi = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("BNK00T","SUBE_ADI", B00C1.BANKA_KODU & B00C1.SUBE_KODU,"KOD+SUBE_KODU")
	AliciHesapNo = B00C1.HESAPNO
	AliciAdres1 = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("CARI00","ADRES_1",AliciCHKodu,"") 
	AliciAdres2 = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("CARI00","ADRES_2",AliciCHKodu,"") 
	AliciAdres3 = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("CARI00","ADRES_3",AliciCHKodu,"") 
	AliciTel = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("CARI00","TELEFONNO_1",AliciCHKodu,"") 
	AliciFaks = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("CARI00","FAXNO",AliciCHKodu,"") 
	AliciVergiDairesi = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("CARI00","VERGIDAIRESI",AliciCHKodu,"")
	AliciVergiNo = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("CARI00","VERGINO",AliciCHKodu,"") 

	if B00C1.BANKA_KODU = B00C.BANKA_KODU Then
		IslemTipi = "HAVALE"
	Else
		IslemTipi = "EFT"
	End if

	res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 9)
	res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")

	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(UstMargin+32, SolMargin, 6,37,"Banka Numarasý")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + 6, pe1.GetLeft, 6,37, BankaCHKodu)

	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(UstMargin+32, pe1.GetRight, 6,37,"Ýþlem Numarasý")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + 6, pe1.GetLeft, 6,37, trim(CARI20E.EVRAKNO) & "/" & CARI20T.TRNUM(satirNo))

	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(UstMargin+32, pe1.GetRight, 6,37,"Ýþlem Tipi")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + 6, pe1.GetLeft, 6,37, IslemTipi)

	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(UstMargin+32, pe1.GetRight, 6,37,"Ýþlem Tarihi")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + 6, pe1.GetLeft, 6,37, Tarih)

	res = pe1.Line(UstMargin + 47, solMargin, 0, sagMargin - solMargin)


	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 14)
	res = pe1.Write(UstMargin + 55, solMargin, 6,sagMargin - solMargin, BankaAdi)
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 9)
	res = pe1.Write(UstMargin + 60, solMargin, 6,sagMargin - solMargin, trim(SubeAdi) & " ÞUBESÝ")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi, solMargin, 6,sagMargin - solMargin, Adres1)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi, solMargin, 6,sagMargin - solMargin, Adres2)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi, solMargin, 6,sagMargin - solMargin, Adres3)

	Gen1 = 15
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + 2 * satirYuksekligi, solMargin, 6,Gen1, "Telefon")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight, 6,Gen1, ": " & Tel)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi, solMargin, 6,Gen1, "Faks")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight, 6,Gen1, ": " & Faks)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi, solMargin, 6,Gen1, "e-mail")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight, 6,Gen1, ": " & email)

	Gen2 = 104
	Blok1 = solMargin + Gen2
	Gen21 = sagMargin - Blok1

	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + 2 * satirYuksekligi, solMargin, 6,Gen2, "Gönderen")
	GonderenUst = pe1.GetTop()
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi, solMargin, 6, Gen2, "Altýn Kablo Sanayii A.Þ.")

	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + 2 * satirYuksekligi, solMargin, 6,Gen2, "Banka ve Þubesi")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi, solMargin, 6, Gen2, trim(BankaAdi) & " / " & trim(SubeAdi))
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi, solMargin, 6, Gen2, HesapNo)

	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + 2 * satirYuksekligi, solMargin, 6,Gen2, "Vergi Dairesi ve No")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi, solMargin, 6, Gen2, trim(VergiDairesi) & " / " & trim(VergiNo))

'ALICI
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(GonderenUst, Blok1, 6,Gen21, "Alýcý")
	GonderenUst = pe1.GetTop()
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi, pe1.GetLeft, 6, Gen21, trim(AliciCHAdi))

	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + 2 * satirYuksekligi, pe1.GetLeft, 6,Gen21, "Banka ve Þubesi")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi, pe1.GetLeft, 6, Gen21, trim(AliciBankaAdi) & " / " & trim(AliciSubeAdi) & " Þb.")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi, pe1.GetLeft, 6, Gen21, trim(AliciHesapNo) & " Hs.")

	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + 2 * satirYuksekligi, pe1.GetLeft, 6,Gen21, "Vergi Dairesi ve No")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi, pe1.GetLeft, 6, Gen21, trim(AliciVergiDairesi) & " / " & trim(AliciVergiNo))

	res = pe1.Line(pe1.GetBottom + 5, solMargin, 0, sagMargin - solMargin)

	Gen3 = 29
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi, solMargin, 5, Gen3, "TUTAR")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight(), 5, sagMargin - pe1.GetRight(), ": " & Doc.NumFormat(CARI20T.CH_ALACAKFIYAT(satirNo), 20, 2) & " " & trim(CARI20T.CH_KUR(satirNo)))

	if trim(CARI20T.CH_KUR(satirNo)) = trim(Doc.DefaultPB) Then
		Yaziyla = Doc.NumToText_TL(CARI20T.CH_ALACAKFIYAT(satirNo))
	Else
		Yaziyla = Doc.NumToText(CARI20T.CH_ALACAKFIYAT(satirNo)) & trim(CARI20T.CH_KUR(satirNo))
	End if
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi, solMargin, 5, Gen3, "TUTAR(Yazýyla)")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight(), 5, sagMargin - pe1.GetRight(), ": " & Yaziyla)

	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi, solMargin, 5, Gen3, "Açýklama")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight(), 10, sagMargin - pe1.GetRight(), ": " & trim(CARI20T.ACIKLAMA(satirNo)) & " " & trim(CARI20T.ACIKLAMA2(satirNo)))

	DipNot = "Yukarýda belirtilen iþlemimizin yapýlmasýný, yapýlan iþlemin bankanýzca gerçekleþtirildiðinin kaþe ve " & _
					"imza ile teyidini rica ederiz." & vbcrlf & "Saygýlarýmýzla,"
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetBottom + 12, solMargin, 15, sagMargin - solMargin, DipNot)



	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 12)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetBottom + 5, solMargin, 8, 80, "Altýn Kablo Sanayii A.Þ.")
	res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, sagMargin - 120, 8, 110, trim(BankaAdi))

	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 10)
	KullaniciAdi = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("UDEF", "AD_SOYAD", Doc.Getd7UserName, "")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + 23, solMargin, 8, sagMargin - solMargin, KullaniciAdi)

	

	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 7)
	res = pe1.SetItalic(1)
	DokumanNo = "Kalite Yönetim Sistemi; Doküman No : F71; 05.02.2004; Rev/Baský : 0/1"
	res = pe1.Write(AltMargin, solMargin, 5, sagMargin - solMargin, DokumanNo)
	res = pe1.SetItalic(0)
End Sub

Public sayfaBoyu, sayfaEni, UstMargin, SolMargin, SagMargin, AltMargin, satirYuksekligi
Sub PrintForm()
	HVEFT0T.SafePack()
	Set ss = Doc.CreateSObject("BATCHERRORMODE")
	sayfaBoyu = 297
	sayfaEni = 210

	UstMargin = 30
	AltMargin = SayfaBoyu - 30
	SolMargin = 10
	SagMargin = SayfaEni - 13
	satiryuksekligi = 4
	
	Set pe1 = Doc.CreatePrintEngineOutput()
	res = pe1.Open(0, "Banka Islem Formu-" & trim(HVEFT0E.EVRAKNO), SayfaBoyu, SayfaEni, "")

	satirSayisi = HVEFT0T.GetRecCount()
	For i = 1 To satirSayisi
		if i > 1 Then pe1.PageBreak
		Call Print_form1(pe1, i)
	Next
	res = pe1.Show()
	res = Doc.ActivateAfterMacro(1)
End Sub

Sub Print_form1(pe1, satirNo)
	res = pe1.nopen()
        
	res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 13)
	res = pe1.setalign("CENTER")
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(UstMargin,SolMargin,10, sagMargin - solMargin,"Banka Ýþlem Formu")

	EvrakNo = HVEFT0E.EVRAKNO
	Tarih = Doc.StrtoNordate(HVEFT0E.ODEMETAR)

	AliciCHKodu = HVEFT0T.CH_KODU(satirNo)

	BankaAdi = HVEFT0E.BANKA_BNK00_AD
	SubeAdi = HVEFT0E.SUBE_BNK00T_SUBE_ADI
	HesapNo = HVEFT0E.HESAPNO
	BankaCHKodu = GetBankaCHKodu(HVEFT0E.BANKA, HVEFT0E.SUBE, HVEFT0E.HESAPNO)
	GonderenIBAN = GetBankaIBAN(HVEFT0E.BANKA, HVEFT0E.SUBE, HVEFT0E.HESAPNO)

	Adres1 = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("CARI00","ADRES_1",BankaCHKodu,"") 
	Adres2 = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("CARI00","ADRES_2",BankaCHKodu,"") 
	Adres3 = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("CARI00","ADRES_3",BankaCHKodu,"") 
	Tel = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("CARI00","TELEFONNO_1",BankaCHKodu,"") 
	Faks = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("CARI00","FAXNO",BankaCHKodu,"") 
	VergiDairesi = "Büyük Mükellefler V.D."
	VergiNo = "0610025337"
	email = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("CARI00","EMAIL",BankaCHKodu,"")

	AliciBankaAdi = HVEFT0T.HEDEFBANKA_BNK00_AD(satirNo)
	AliciSubeAdi = HVEFT0T.HEDEFSUBE_BNK00T_SUBE_ADI(satirNo)
	AliciHesapNo = HVEFT0T.HEDEFHESAP(satirNo)
'	AliciIBAN = HVEFT0T.HEDEFIBAN(satirNo)
	AliciIBAN = GetHedefIBAN(AliciCHKodu, HVEFT0T.HEDEFBANKA(satirNo), HVEFT0T.HEDEFSUBE(satirNo), HVEFT0T.HEDEFHESAP(satirNo))
	if Left(AliciCHKodu, 1) = "B" Then
		AliciCHAdi = "ALTIN KABLO SANAYÝÝ A.Þ."
		AliciAdres1 = ""
		AliciAdres2 = ""
		AliciAdres3 = ""
		AliciTel = "" 
		AliciFaks = ""
		AliciVergiDairesi = "BÜYÜK MÜKELLEFLER V.D."
		AliciVergiNo = "0610025337"
		AliciIBAN = GetBankaIBAN2(HVEFT0T.CH_KODU(satirNo))
	Else
		AliciCHAdi = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("CARI00","AD",AliciCHKodu,"")
		AliciAdres1 = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("CARI00","ADRES_1",AliciCHKodu,"") 
		AliciAdres2 = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("CARI00","ADRES_2",AliciCHKodu,"") 
		AliciAdres3 = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("CARI00","ADRES_3",AliciCHKodu,"") 
		AliciTel = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("CARI00","TELEFONNO_1",AliciCHKodu,"") 
		AliciFaks = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("CARI00","FAXNO",AliciCHKodu,"") 
		AliciVergiDairesi = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("CARI00","VERGIDAIRESI",AliciCHKodu,"")
		AliciVergiNo = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("CARI00","VERGINO",AliciCHKodu,"") 
	End if
	if trim(HVEFT0T.HEDEFBANKA(satirNo)) = trim(HVEFT0E.BANKA) Then
		IslemTipi = "HAVALE"
	Else
		IslemTipi = "EFT"
	End if

	res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 9)
	res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")

	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(UstMargin+32, SolMargin, 6,37,"Banka Numarasý")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + 6, pe1.GetLeft, 6,37, BankaCHKodu)

	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(UstMargin+32, pe1.GetRight, 6,37,"Ýþlem Numarasý")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + 6, pe1.GetLeft, 6,37, trim(HVEFT0E.EVRAKNO) & "/" & HVEFT0T.TRNUM(satirNo))

	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(UstMargin+32, pe1.GetRight, 6,37,"Ýþlem Tipi")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + 6, pe1.GetLeft, 6,37, IslemTipi)

	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(UstMargin+32, pe1.GetRight, 6,37,"Ýþlem Tarihi")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + 6, pe1.GetLeft, 6,37, Tarih)

	res = pe1.Line(UstMargin + 47, solMargin, 0, sagMargin - solMargin)


	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 14)
	res = pe1.Write(UstMargin + 55, solMargin, 6,sagMargin - solMargin, BankaAdi)
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 9)
	res = pe1.Write(UstMargin + 60, solMargin, 6,sagMargin - solMargin, trim(SubeAdi) & " ÞUBESÝ")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi, solMargin, 6,sagMargin - solMargin, Adres1)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi, solMargin, 6,sagMargin - solMargin, Adres2)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi, solMargin, 6,sagMargin - solMargin, Adres3)

	Gen1 = 15
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + 2 * satirYuksekligi, solMargin, 6,Gen1, "Telefon")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight, 6,Gen1+5, ": " & Tel)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi, solMargin, 6,Gen1, "Faks")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight, 6,Gen1+5, ": " & Faks)

Function GetBankaCHKodu(BankaKodu, SubeKodu, HesapNo)
	Set I00 = Doc.Select1("sewe333", "IDARI00B", "BANKA_KODU='" & BankaKodu &"'&SUBE_KODU='" & SubeKodu & "'&HESAPNO='" & HesapNo & "'", "", -1)
	if I00.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
		GetBankaCHKodu = I00.CH_KODU
	End if
End Function
Function GetBankaIBAN(BankaKodu, SubeKodu, HesapNo)
	Set I00 = Doc.Select1("sewe333", "IDARI00B", "BANKA_KODU='" & BankaKodu &"'&SUBE_KODU='" & SubeKodu & "'&HESAPNO='" & HesapNo & "'", "", -1)
	if I00.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
		GetBankaIBAN = I00.IBAN
	End if
End Function
Function GetBankaIBAN2(CHKodu)
	Set I00 = Doc.Select1("sewe333", "IDARI00B", "Ch_Kodu='" & CHKodu &"'", "", -1)
	if I00.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
		GetBankaIBAN2 = I00.IBAN
	End if
End Function

Function GetHedefIBAN(CHKodu, BankaKodu, SubeKodu, HesapNo)
'	MSGBOX CHKodu & ", " & BankaKodu & ", " & SubeKodu & ", " & HesapNo
	Set B00C = Doc.Select1("dfr56yh788", "BNK00C", "KOD='" & CHKodu & "'&BANKA_KODU='" & BankaKodu & "'&SUBE_KODU='" & SubeKodu & "'&HESAPNO='" & HesapNo & "'", "", -1)
'	msgbox B00C.GetRowCount()
	if B00C.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
		GetHedefIBAN = B00C.IBAN
	End if
End Function

Form Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : AK2010 / Uygulama : PFRM7S
Makro Kodu : CARI00TV-1
Hangi Tablo için : CARI00TV
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : 
Açýklama : Cari Hesap Bakiye Yaþlandýrma Listesi
Kod : 
Public sayfaBoyu, sayfaEni, UstMargin, SolMargin, SagMargin, AltMargin, satirYuksekligi
Dim Grid, siraNo, satirBaslangic
Sub PrintForm()
	sayfaBoyu = 297
	sayfaEni = 210

	UstMargin = 25
	AltMargin = SayfaBoyu - 30
	SolMargin = 10
	SagMargin = SayfaEni - 10
	satiryuksekligi = 5
	siraNo = 0
		
	Call Alanlar()
	Set pe1 = Doc.CreatePrintEngineOutput()
	res = pe1.Open(0, "Bakiye Yaslandirma Listesi-" & trim(CARI00.KOD), SayfaBoyu, SayfaEni, "")

	if DT05.GetRowCount() = 0 Then Exit Sub
	Call SayfaBasi(pe1)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 10)
	For i = 1 To DT05.GetRowCount()
		Call SatirYazdir(pe1, i)
	Next
	Call SayfaSonuYazdir(pe1)
	res = pe1.Show()
	res = Doc.ActivateAfterMacro(1)
End Sub
Sub SayfaBasi(pe1)
	FirmaAdi = Doc.Customp_Gets("FNAMEL0")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 12)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(UstMargin, solMargin, 5, 100, FirmaAdi)

	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 14)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + 15, solMargin, 7, 50, "C/H Bakiye Listesi")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 10)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, sagMargin - 50, 5, 50, "Tarih : " & Doc.StrToNorDate(Doc.BuGun))
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")

	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 10)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + 10, solMargin, 5, 150, "Firma : " & trim(CARI00.KOD) & " - " & CARI00.AD)

	satirBaslangic = pe1.GetTop + 20

	sol = solMargin + 1
	For i = 0 to uBound(Grid, 1)
		if Grid(i,4) = "N" Then
			pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
		Else
			pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
		End if
		res = pe1.Write(satirBaslangic - 12, sol, 10, Grid(i,1) - 2, Grid(i,2))
		sol = pe1.GetRight() + 2
	Next
	res = pe1.Line(satirBaslangic - 13, solMargin, 0, sagMargin - solMargin)
	res = pe1.Line(satirBaslangic - 1, solMargin, 0, sagMargin - solMargin)
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
End Sub
Sub SatirYazdir(pe1, satirNo)
	if pe1.GetBottom >= AltMargin Then
		'Call SayfaSonuYazdir(pe1)
		res = pe1.PageBreak()
		siraNo = 0
	End if
	siraNo = siraNo + 1
	satirPos = satirBaslangic + (siraNo - 1) * satirYuksekligi

	sol = solMargin + 1
	For i = 0 to UBound(Grid, 1)
		res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
		AlanAdi = Grid(i,0)
		SutunGenisligi = Grid(i,1)
		if AlanAdi = "_PB" Then
			AlanDegeri = Doc.DefaultPB()
		Else
			AlanDegeri = DT05.GetFieldValue(AlanAdi, satirNo)
		End if
		If Grid(i,4) = "N" Then
			res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
			Ondalik = Grid(i,6)
			AlanDegeri = Doc.NumFormat(AlanDegeri, 18,Ondalik)
		ElseIf Grid(i,4) = "D" Then
			AlanDegeri = Doc.StrToNorDate(AlanDegeri)
		End If			
		res = pe1.write(satirPos, Sol, satiryuksekligi, SutunGenisligi - 2, AlanDegeri)
		
		Sol = Sol + SutunGenisligi						
	Next
End Sub
Sub SayfaSonuYazdir(pe1)
	res = pe1.Line(pe1.GetBottom, solMargin, 0, sagMargin - solMargin)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	solTarih = solMargin + Grid(0,1) + Grid(1,1) + Grid(2,1) + 1
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + 1, solTarih, 5, Grid(3,1) - 2, "Ort. Vade")
	OrtalamaVade = Doc.StrDate_Increment(TL_YASLANDIRMA_KRITERLERI.HESAPLAMATARIHI, DT04.A004)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 2, 5, Grid(4,1) - 2, Doc.StrtoNorDate(OrtalamaVade))
	res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 2, 5, Grid(5,1) - 2, Doc.NumFormat(DT04.A004,6,2))
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 2, 5, Grid(6,1) - 2, Doc.NumFormat(DT04.A002, 18,2))
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 2, 5, Grid(7,1) - 2, Doc.DefaultPB)
End Sub
Sub Alanlar()
	ReDim Grid(7, 6)
	Grid(0,0) = "ETN"
	Grid(1,0) = "EVRAKNO"
	Grid(2,0) = "ACIKLAMA"
	Grid(3,0) = "TARIH"
	Grid(4,0) = "VALORTARIH"
	Grid(5,0) = "VALORGUN"
	Grid(6,0) = "TUTAR"
	Grid(7,0) = "_PB"

	Grid(0,1) = 15
	Grid(1,1) = 20
	Grid(2,1) = 55
	Grid(3,1) = 22
	Grid(4,1) = 22
	Grid(5,1) = 20
	Grid(6,1) = 25
	Grid(7,1) = 13

	Grid(0,2) = "Evrak Türü"
	Grid(1,2) = "Evrak No"
	Grid(2,2) = "Açýklama"
	Grid(3,2) = "Ýþlem Tarihi"
	Grid(4,2) = "Valör Tarihi"
	Grid(5,2) = "Valör Gün"
	Grid(6,2) = "Orijinal Hareket Tutarý"
	Grid(7,2) = "Para Birimi"

	Grid(0,4) = "C"
	Grid(1,4) = "C"
	Grid(2,4) = "C"
	Grid(3,4) = "D"
	Grid(4,4) = "D"
	Grid(5,4) = "N"
	Grid(6,4) = "N"
	Grid(7,4) = "C"

	Grid(5,6) = 0
	Grid(6,6) = 2
End Sub

Sub KangalEtiketi()
	sayfaBoyu	= 60
	sayfaEni = 80
	UstMargin = 2
	SolMargin = 3
	AltMargin = sayfaBoyu - 8
	sagMargin = sayfaEni - 3

	Set pe1 = doc.CreatePrintEngineOutput()
	res = pe1.Open(0,"Kangal Etiketi" & trim(MMPS10E.EVRAKNO), sayfaBoyu, sayfaEni, "")
	res = pe1.SetBarcodeAutoCheckSum(0)
	res = pe1.SetBarcodeParms(2,2)
	
	Call KangalEtiketiYazdir(pe1)

	res = pe1.Show()
	res = doc.ActivateAfterMacro(1)
End Sub
Sub KangalEtiketiYazdir(pe1)
	Set M10E = Doc.GetTableObject("MMPS10E")

	logo = Doc.Getd7Directory_DataFirm & trim(Doc.Customp_Gets("!LOGO"))
' 	Logo = Doc.Getd7Directory_DataFirm & "images\logo2.bmp"
	res = pe1.PenSize(0.3)
	res = pe1.Box(UstMargin, solMargin, AltMargin - UstMargin, sagMargin - solMargin)
	res = pe1.Line(UstMargin + 12, solMargin, 0, sagMargin - solMargin)
	res = pe1.NoPen()
	res = pe1.Picture(UstMargin + 0.5, solmargin + 1, 11,30, logo, 114)
	res = pe1.PenSize(0.3)

'	res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
'	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 16)
'	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
'	res = pe1.Write(UstMargin + 1, solMargin + 10, 8, sagMargin - solMargin - 10, "ALTIN KABLO")
'	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 8)
'	res = pe1.Write(UstMargin + 8, solMargin + 10, 6, sagMargin - solMargin - 10, "SANAYÝÝ ANONÝM ÞÝRKETÝ")
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")

	Gen1 = 20
	Gen2 = 17
	Aralik = 3.7
	Aralik2 = 10
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 7)
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.StoreSet(1)
	res = pe1.Write(UstMargin + 12.5, solMargin + 1, 5, Gen1, "Ürün Kodu")
	satirUst = pe1.GetTop
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.SetItalic(1)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow", 10)
	res = pe1.StoreSet(2)	
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + Aralik, pe1.GetLeft, 5, Gen1, M10E.MAMULSTOKKODU)

	Kesit = Doc.GetUDFValue("STOK00", M10E.MAMULSTOKKODU, "KESIT", 0)
	Cinsi = Doc.GetUDFValue("STOK00", M10E.MAMULSTOKKODU, "CINSI", 0)
	Gerilim = Doc.GetUDFValue("STOK00", M10E.MAMULSTOKKODU, "GERILIM", 0)
	Renk = Doc.GetUDFValue("STOK00", M10E.MAMULSTOKKODU, "RENK", 0)

	res = pe1.UseSet(1)
	res = pe1.Write(satirUst, pe1.GetRight + 1, 5, Gen2, "Kesit(mm2)")
	res = pe1.UseSet(2)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + Aralik, pe1.GetLeft, 5, Gen2, Kesit)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")

	res = pe1.UseSet(1)
	res = pe1.Write(satirUst, pe1.GetRight + 1, 5, sagMargin - pe1.GetRight - 1, "Cinsi")
	res = pe1.UseSet(2)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 8)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + Aralik, pe1.GetLeft, 5, sagMargin - pe1.GetLeft - 1, Cinsi)

	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 7)
	res = pe1.UseSet(1)
	res = pe1.Write(satirUst + Aralik2, solMargin + 1, 5, Gen1, "Standard")
	satirUst = pe1.GetTop
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 8)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + Aralik, pe1.GetLeft, 5, Gen1, Standard)

	res = pe1.UseSet(1)
	res = pe1.Write(satirUst, pe1.GetRight + 1, 5, Gen2, "Gerilim")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + Aralik, pe1.GetLeft, 5, Gen2, Gerilim)

	res = pe1.UseSet(1)
	res = pe1.Write(satirUst, pe1.GetRight + 1, 5, sagMargin - pe1.GetRight - 1, "Renk")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 8)

	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + Aralik, pe1.GetLeft, 5, sagMargin - pe1.GetLeft - 1, Renk)

	res = pe1.Write(satirUst + Aralik2, solMargin + 1, 5, Gen1, "Üretim Tarihi")
	satirUst = pe1.GetTop
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 8)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.StoreSet(2)	
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + Aralik, pe1.GetLeft, 5, Gen1, Doc.StrToNorDate(Doc.BuGun))

	res = pe1.UseSet(1)

	res = pe1.UseSet(2)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + Aralik, pe1.GetLeft, 5, Gen2, trim(M10E.EVRAKNO))

	AltCizgiLen = 8

	res = pe1.Line(pe1.GetTop, solMargin + 45 , AltCizgiLen, 0)

	res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
	res = pe1.Write(AltMargin - 7, solMargin + 42, 8, sagMargin - solMargin - 44, Boy & " m")
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
End Sub

Form Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : AK2010 / Uygulama : PFRM7S

Kod : 
Public Ust, EtiketBoyu, UstMargin, Solmargin, sagMargin, EtiketNo, D7LB
Sub PrintForm()
	Call PrintLabel()
End Sub
Sub PrintLabel()
	set pe1 = Doc.CreatePrintEngineOutput()
	EtiketNo = 0
	sayfaBoyu = 75
	sayfaEni = 210
	Ustmargin = 4
	EtiketBoyu = 70
	EtiketEni = 206
	solMargin = 1
	sagMargin = sayfaEni- 1

	set pe1 = Doc.CreatePrintEngineOutput()
	res = pe1.Open(0,"Etiket", sayfaEni, sayfaBoyu, "LANDSCAPE")
	res = pe1.SetBarcodeAlignment(2)
	
	EtiketFormu = SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME
	Set d7LB = Doc.RunMacro1("MAKR7S.ETIKET", "GetEtiketTable", EtiketFormu)

	For i = 1 to d7LB.GetRowCount()
		if i > 1 Then pe1.PageBreak()
		Call Etiket(pe1, i)
	Next
	if Doc.GetDocType0 = "TRACK25" or Doc.GetDocType0 = "TRACK251" Then
		res = pe1.PrintDoc(1)
	Else
		pe1.Show
	End if
End Sub
Sub Etiket(pe1,satirNo)
	Ust = UstMargin
' dikey çizgilerin yerleri
	D1 = SolMargin + 40
	D2 = SolMargin + 40
	D3 = SolMargin + 90
	D4 = solMargin + 160

'yatay çizgilerin yerleri
	Y1 = Ust + 15
	Y2 = Ust + 20

	res = pe1.NoPen()
	logo = Doc.GetD7Directory_DataFirm & Doc.Customp_Gets("!LOGO")
	res = pe1.Picture(Ust + 1,solMargin + 1, Y1 - Ust - 2,-2,logo, 114)

	satirAra = 6
	res = pe1.PenSize(0.5)
	res = pe1.Box(Ust,SolMargin, EtiketBoyu - 3, sagMargin-SolMargin)
	Alt = pe1.GetBottom()
	res = pe1.PenSize(0.3)
	res = pe1.Line(20,SolMargin,0, SagMargin-1)		'üstteki çizgi
	res = pe1.Line(30,SolMargin,0,D4-SolMargin-1)		'malzeme isminin altýndaki
	res = pe1.Line(40,SolMargin,0,D4-SolMargin-1)
	res = pe1.Line(50,SolMargin,0,D4-SolMargin-1)
	res = pe1.Line(60,SolMargin,0,D4-SolMargin-1)
	Y3 = pe1.GetTop
	res = pe1.PenSize(0.3)
'	res = pe1.Line(Y3,D3,EtiketBoyu - Y3 + 2,0)
'	res = pe1.Line(Ust,D4,Alt - Ust,0)

	StokKodu = D7LB.SKOD(satirNo)
	StokAdi = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00","AD",StokKodu,"")
	Miktar = D7LB.SF_MIKTAR(satirNo)
	Birim = D7LB.SF_UNIT(satirNo)
	CHKodu = D7LB.CH_TEDARIKCI(satirNo)
	CHAdi = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("CARI00","AD",CHKodu,"")
	Tarih = Doc.StrToNorDate(D7LB.REFTARIH1(satirNo))
	IrsaliyeNo = D7LB.EVRAKNO(satirNo)
	PartiNo = D7LB.LOTNUMBER(satirNo)

	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",15)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
	res = pe1.Write(Ust + 2, solMargin + 20, 14, 80, "MALZEME TANITIM KARTI")
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")

	res = pe1.StoreSet(1)
	res = pe1.NoPen()
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",8)
	res = pe1.Barcode(Ust + 2, solMargin + 100, 12, 50, "CODE128", trim(D7LB.KOD(satirNo)), "")

	res = pe1.UseSet(1)

	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",9)
	res = pe1.Write(20, 3, 8, D1 - solMargin - 2, "Stok Adý")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",16)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, D1 + 1, 10, D4 - D1 - 2, StokAdi)

	res = pe1.Line(20,160,52,0)

	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",9)
	res = pe1.Write(31, 3, 8, D1 - solMargin - 2, "Stok Kodu")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",16)
	res = pe1.Write(31, D1 + 1, 8, D4 - D1 - 2, StokKodu)

	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",9)
	res = pe1.Write(41, 3, 8, 35, "Miktar")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",16)
	res = pe1.Write(41, D1 + 1, 8, 35, Doc.NumFormat(Miktar,18,Doc.SigDig(Miktar)) & " " & Birim)


'	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",9)
	res = pe1.Write(51, 3, 8, 35, "Lot No")
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",16)
	res = pe1.Write(51, D1 + 1, 8, 35, trim(PartiNo))

	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",8)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(61, 3, 8, 35, "Mlz.Türü")
	GK1 = Doc.getDBFieldValue("STOK00", "GK_1", StokKodu, "")
	GK1Adi = Doc.getGecoValue("STKGK1", "AD", GK1, -1)
	res = pe1.Write(61, D1 + 1, 8, sagMargin - pe1.GetRight -1, GK1Adi)
' 	res = pe1.Line(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetLeft-1, 5.8, 0)

' 	tarih bölümü
'	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",8)
	res = pe1.Write(61, 100, 8, 35, "Tarih")
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",16)
	res = pe1.Write(61, 120, 8, 35, Tarih) 

	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",12)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(22, D4 + 1, 8, sagMargin - D4 - 2, " Lokasyon :")

	L1 = d7LB.LOCATION1(satirNo)
	L2 = d7LB.LOCATION2(satirNo)
	L3 = d7LB.LOCATION3(satirNo)
	L4 = d7LB.LOCATION4(satirNo)
	Lokasyon = trim(L1) & "-" & trim(L2) & trim(L3) & trim(L4)

	res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",30)
	res = pe1.Write(40, 162,30,30,trim(Lokasyon))
'	res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
'	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",8)
'	res = pe1.Line(pe1.GetBottom, D4, 0, sagMargin - D4)

	Aralik = 12

	res = pe1.PenSize(0.2)
'	res = pe1.Line(Ust1 - 1, D4, 0, sagMargin - D4)
'	res = pe1.Line(pe1.GetTop + Aralik, D4, 0, sagMargin - D4)

	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",7)
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.Write(Alt + 0.2, solMargin + 1, 3, 40, "Form No : 01.01 - FR20")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight, 3, 52, "Yayýn Tarihi : 30/07/2004")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight, 3, 23, "Rev.No : 01")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight, 3, 30, "Rev.Tarihi : 17/09/2004")
End Sub

Form Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : AK2010 / Uygulama : PFRM7S
Makro Kodu : ETK-PALET

Public 	sayfaBoyu, sayfaEni, UstMargin, SolMargin, AltMargin, sagMargin, S19T
Sub PrintLabel()
	Call PrintForm()
End Sub
Sub PrintForm()
	Set s = Doc.CreateSObject("BATCHERRORMODE")

	sayfaBoyu	= 297
	sayfaEni = 210
	UstMargin = 20
	SolMargin = 20
	AltMargin = UstMargin + 180
	sagMargin = solMargin + 162.5

	Set pe1 = doc.CreatePrintEngineOutput()
	res = pe1.Open(0,"Palet Etiketi" & trim(STOK19E.EVRAKNO), sayfaBoyu, sayfaEni, "")
	res = pe1.SetBarcodeAutoCheckSum(0)
	res = pe1.SetBarcodeParms(2,2)
	Set S19T = Doc.Select2("se3333", "STOK19T", "", "", -1)
	S19T.GroupBy2("PACKNO_1+ALLOC_SIPARTNO")
	S19T.SafePack()
	For i = 1 to S19T.GetRowCount()
		siraNo = siraNo  + 1
		if siraNo  > 1 Then res = pe1.PageBreak()
		Call PaletEtiketiYazdir(pe1, i)
	Next
	res = pe1.Show()
	res = doc.ActivateAfterMacro(1)
End Sub
Sub PaletEtiketiYazdir(pe1, satirNo)
	Set E = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK19E")

	Y1 = UstMargin + 19
	D1 = solMargin + 64.5
	res = pe1.PenSize(0.3)
	res = pe1.Box(UstMargin, solMargin, AltMargin - UstMargin, sagMargin - solMargin)
	res = pe1.Line(Y1, solMargin, 0, sagMargin - solMargin)
	res = pe1.Line(UstMargin, D1, Y1 - UstMargin, 0)

	Gen1 = 47
	Aralik = 11
	Aralik1 = 9
	Aralik2 = 14
	Aralik3 = 17

	PaletNo = S19T.PACKNO_1(satirNo)
	MusteriSiparisNo = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK40E", "CHSIPNO", S19T.ALLOC_SIPNO(satirNo), "")
	CHKodu = STOK19E.CHKODU
	MusteriStokKodu = Doc.GetCHStokKodu(S19T.KOD(satirNo), CHKodu)
	if trim(MusteriStokKodu) = "" Then MusteriStokKodu = S19T.KOD(satirNo)
	MusteriStokAdi = Doc.GetCHStokAdi(S19T.KOD(satirNo), CHKodu)
	if trim(MusteriStokAdi) = "" Then MusteriStokAdi = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", S19T.KOD(satirNo), "")
	SupplierNo = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("CARI00", "TEXT_TECH_1", CHKodu, "")
	SipArtNo  = S19T.ALLOC_SIPARTNO(satirNo)
	StokKodu = S19T.KOD(satirNo)
'	TotalPacks = Doc.GetUDFValue("STOK00", StokKodu, "MIKTAR", 0)
	KangalBoyu = Doc.GetUDFValue("STOK00", StokKodu, "BOY", 0)
	TotalPacks = Doc.Div(S19T.SF_MIKTAR(satirNo), KangalBoyu)
	QtyPcsInPack = 1
	QtyPcsInUnit = TotalPacks * QtyPcsInPack
	PaletYerlesimi = Doc.GetUDFValue("STOK00", StokKodu, "YERLESIM", 0)
	CableLength = S19T.SF_MIKTAR(satirNo)
	par1 = S19T.KOD(satirNo)
	par2 = S19T.SF_MIKTAR(satirNo)
	NetAgirlik = Doc.RunMacro2("MAKR7S.NETAGIRLIK", "GetNetAgirlik", par1, par2)
	NetAgirlik = Doc.Round(NetAgirlik, 0)
	BrutAgirlik = NetAgirlik + 40


	res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow", 44)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(UstMargin, solMargin, 22, D1 - solMargin, trim(PaletNo))

	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 18)
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.Write(UstMargin + 6, D1, 8, sagMargin - D1, "PALLET")

	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 12)
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.StoreSet(1)
	res = pe1.Write(Y1 + 9, solMargin + 3.5, 6, Gen1, "Customer Name")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 6, 5, ":")
	satirUst = pe1.GetTop
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow", 24)
	res = pe1.StoreSet(2)	
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop - 2, pe1.GetRight + 2, 12, sagMargin - pe1.GetRight - 2, STOK19E.CHKODU_CARI00_AD)

	res = pe1.UseSet(1)
	res = pe1.Write(satirUst + Aralik, solMargin + 3, 6, Gen1, "Customer Order No")
	satirUst = pe1.GetTop
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 6, 5, ":")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 14)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 2, 7, sagMargin - pe1.GetRight - 2, MusteriSiparisNo)

	res = pe1.UseSet(1)
	res = pe1.Write(satirUst + Aralik, solMargin + 3, 6, Gen1, "Customer Product Code")
	satirUst = pe1.GetTop
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 6, 5, ":")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 16)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 2, 7, sagMargin - pe1.GetRight - 2, MusteriStokKodu)

	res = pe1.UseSet(1)
	res = pe1.Write(satirUst + Aralik, solMargin + 3, 6, Gen1, "Customer Product Desc.")
	satirUst = pe1.GetTop
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 6, 5, ":")
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow", 16)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 2, 7, sagMargin - pe1.GetRight - 2, MusteriStokAdi)

	res = pe1.UseSet(1)
	res = pe1.Write(satirUst + Aralik2, solMargin + 3, 6, Gen1, "Supplier No")
	satirUst = pe1.GetTop
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 6, 5, ":")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 12)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 2, 7, sagMargin - pe1.GetRight - 2, SupplierNo)

	res = pe1.UseSet(1)
	res = pe1.Write(satirUst + Aralik1, solMargin + 3, 6, Gen1, "Supplier Order/Line No")
	satirUst = pe1.GetTop
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 6, 5, ":")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 12)

	res = pe1.UseSet(1)

	satirUst = pe1.GetTop

	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 12)

	res = pe1.UseSet(1)

	satirUst = pe1.GetTop
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 6, 5, ":")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 12)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 2, 7, sagMargin - pe1.GetRight - 2, QtyPcsInPack)

	res = pe1.UseSet(1)
	res = pe1.Write(satirUst + Aralik1, solMargin + 3, 6, Gen1, "Qty Pcs In Unit")
	satirUst = pe1.GetTop
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 6, 5, ":")

	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 2, 7, 50, QtyPcsInUnit)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 2, 7, sagMargin - pe1.GetRight - 2, "(" & trim(PaletYerlesimi) & ")")

	res = pe1.UseSet(1)
	res = pe1.Write(satirUst + Aralik1, solMargin + 3, 6, Gen1, "Cable Length (m)")
	satirUst = pe1.GetTop
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 6, 5, ":")
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 12)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 2, 7, sagMargin - pe1.GetRight - 2, CableLength)

	res = pe1.UseSet(1)

	satirUst = pe1.GetTop
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 6, 5, ":")
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 12)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 2, 7, sagMargin - pe1.GetRight - 2, NetAgirlik)

	res = pe1.UseSet(1)
	res = pe1.Write(satirUst + Aralik1, solMargin + 3, 6, Gen1, "Gross Weight")
	satirUst = pe1.GetTop
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 6, 5, ":")
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 12)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 2, 7, sagMargin - pe1.GetRight - 2, BrutAgirlik)

	res = pe1.NoPen()
	res = pe1.Barcode(pe1.GetTop + 10, solMargin + 50, 10, 70, "CODE39", trim(PaletNo), "")
End Sub
Function GetNetAgirlik(StokKodu, Miktar)
	ret=Doc.Calc_Bom("O","M",StokKodu,1,Miktar,"","","","","","","","")  	 	
	Set Tbmrsys =Doc.GetTableObject("_WBMRSYS_SELECT") 		
	For i = 1 to Tbmrsys.GetRowCount()	
		if Tbmrsys.BOMREC_INPUTTYPE(i) = "H" Then
			HammaddeKodu = Tbmrsys.BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE(i)
			Birim = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "IUNIT", HammaddeKodu, "")
			if trim(uCase(Birim)) = "KG" Then
				ToplamAgirlik = ToplamAgirlik + Tbmrsys.BOMREC_KAYNAK0(i)
			End if
		End if
	Next
	GetNetAgirlik = ToplamAgirlik
End Function

Public 	sayfaBoyu, sayfaEni, UstMargin, SolMargin, AltMargin, sagMargin, S19T
Sub PrintLabel()
	Call PrintForm()
End Sub
Sub PrintForm()
	sayfaBoyu	= 297
	sayfaEni = 210
	UstMargin = 20
	SolMargin = 20
	AltMargin = UstMargin + 180
	sagMargin = solMargin + 162.5

	Set pe1 = doc.CreatePrintEngineOutput()
	res = pe1.Open(0,"Palet Etiketi" & trim(STOK19E.EVRAKNO), sayfaBoyu, sayfaEni, "")
	res = pe1.SetBarcodeAutoCheckSum(0)
	res = pe1.SetBarcodeParms(2,2)
	Set S19T = Doc.Select2("se3333", "STOK19T", "", "", -1)
	S19T.GroupBy2("PACKNO_1+ALLOC_SIPARTNO")
	S19T.SafePack()
	For i = 1 to S19T.GetRowCount()
		siraNo = siraNo  + 1
		if siraNo  > 1 Then res = pe1.PageBreak()
		Call PaletEtiketiYazdir(pe1, i)
	Next
	res = pe1.Show()
	res = doc.ActivateAfterMacro(1)
End Sub
Sub PaletEtiketiYazdir(pe1, satirNo)
	Set E = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK19E")

	Y1 = UstMargin + 19
	D1 = solMargin + 64.5

	res = pe1.Box(UstMargin, solMargin, AltMargin - UstMargin, sagMargin - solMargin)
	res = pe1.Line(Y1, solMargin, 0, sagMargin - solMargin)
	res = pe1.Line(UstMargin, D1, Y1 - UstMargin, 0)

	Gen1 = 47
	Aralik = 11
	Aralik1 = 9
	Aralik2 = 14
	Aralik3 = 17

	MusteriStokAdi = Doc.GetCHStokAdi(S19T.KOD(satirNo), CHKodu)
	if trim(MusteriStokAdi) = "" Then MusteriStokAdi = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", S19T.KOD(satirNo), "")
	SupplierNo = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("CARI00", "TEXT_TECH_1", CHKodu, "")

	StokKodu = S19T.KOD(satirNo)
'	TotalPacks = Doc.GetUDFValue("STOK00", StokKodu, "MIKTAR", 0)
	KangalBoyu = Doc.GetUDFValue("STOK00", StokKodu, "BOY", 0)
	TotalPacks = Doc.Div(S19T.SF_MIKTAR(satirNo), KangalBoyu)
	QtyPcsInPack = 1
	QtyPcsInUnit = TotalPacks * QtyPcsInPack
	PaletYerlesimi = Doc.GetUDFValue("STOK00", StokKodu, "YERLESIM", 0)

Dim sayfaBoyu, sayfaEni, UstMargin, SolMargin, AltMargin, sagMargin
Sub PrintForm()
	Call PrintLabel()
End Sub
Sub PrintLabel()
	sayfaBoyu	= 80 + 7
	sayfaEni = 100
	UstMargin = 11
	SolMargin = 3
	AltMargin = sayfaBoyu - 3
	sagMargin = sayfaEni - 3

	Set pe1 = doc.CreatePrintEngineOutput()
	res = pe1.Open(0,"Hammadde Tanitim Karti" & trim(STOK29E.EVRAKNO), sayfaBoyu, sayfaEni, "")

	STOK29T.SafePack()
	For i = 1 to STOK29T.GetRowCount()
		if i > 1 Then pe1.PageBreak
		Call YMamulTanitimKartiYazdir(pe1, i)
	Next
	pe1.Show
End Sub
Sub YMamulTanitimKartiYazdir(pe1, satirNo)
	logo = Doc.Getd7Directory_DataFirm & trim(Doc.Customp_Gets("!LOGO"))
'	logo = Doc.Getd7Directory_DataFirm & "images\logo2.bmp"
	res = pe1.PenSize(0.3)
	res = pe1.Box(UstMargin, solMargin, AltMargin - UstMargin, sagMargin - solMargin)
	res = pe1.Line(UstMargin + 11, solMargin, 0, sagMargin - solMargin)
	res = pe1.NoPen()
	res = pe1.Picture(UstMargin + 0.5, solmargin + 1, 10,30, logo, 114)
	res = pe1.PenSize(0.3)

	res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 12)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(UstMargin + 0.5, solMargin + 35, 15, sagMargin - solMargin - 40, "HAMMADDE TANITIM KARTI")
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")

	Gen1 = 17
	Aralik = 5
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 9)
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.Write(UstMargin + 12, solMargin + 1, 5, Gen1, "Kod")
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow", 12)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 5, sagMargin - pe1.GetRight - 2, ": " & STOK29T.KOD(satirNo))

	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 9)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + Aralik, solMargin + 1, 5, Gen1, "Taným")
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow", 12)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 5, sagMargin - pe1.GetRight - 2, ": " & Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", STOK29T.KOD(satirNo), ""))

	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 9)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + Aralik, solMargin + 1, 5, Gen1, "Tedarikçi")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 5, sagMargin - pe1.GetRight - 2, ": " & Doc.GetDBFieldValue("CARI00", "AD", STOK29E.CARIHESAPCODE, ""))

	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + Aralik, solMargin + 1, 5, Gen1, "Giriþ Tarihi")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 5, sagMargin - pe1.GetRight - 2, ": " & Doc.StrToNorDate(STOK29E.TARIH))

	YaziAlt = pe1.GetBottom()

	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + Aralik, solMargin + 1, 5, Gen1, "Lot No")
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 12)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 6, 30, ": " & STOK29T.LOTNUMBER(satirNo))

	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 6, 14, "Miktar")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 6, sagMargin - pe1.GetRight - 2, ": " & Doc.NumFormat(STOK29T.SF_MIKTAR(satirNo),18,Doc.SigDig(STOK29T.SF_MIKTAR(satirNo))) & " " & trim(STOK29T.SF_SF_UNIT(satirNo)))

	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 9)
	res = pe1.NoPen()
	res = pe1.Barcode(pe1.GetTop + 10, solMargin + 3, 10, 50, "CODE39", trim(STOK29T.LOTNUMBER(satirNo)), "")

	res = pe1.PenSize(0.3)
	res = pe1.Line(YaziAlt + 7, solMargin + 58, 0, sagMargin - solMargin - 58)
	Ust = pe1.GetTop
	sol = pe1.GetLeft
	res = pe1.Line(pe1.GetTop + 4, pe1.GetLeft, 0, sagMargin - pe1.GetLeft)
	res = pe1.Line(Ust, sol, AltMargin - Ust, 0)

	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 9)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
	res = pe1.Write(Ust + 0.3, sol, 5, sagMargin - sol, "KALÝTE KONTROL")
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
End Sub

Makro Kodu : HMDETIKET1

Kod : 
Dim sayfaBoyu, sayfaEni, UstMargin, SolMargin, AltMargin, sagMargin
Sub PrintForm()
	Call PrintLabel()
End Sub
Sub PrintLabel()
	sayfaBoyu	= 80
	sayfaEni = 100
	UstMargin = 4
	SolMargin = 3
	AltMargin = sayfaBoyu - 3
	sagMargin = sayfaEni - 3

	Set pe1 = doc.CreatePrintEngineOutput()
	res = pe1.Open(0,"Hammadde Tanitim Karti" & trim(STOK68E.EVRAKNO), sayfaBoyu, sayfaEni, "")

	STOK68T.SafePack()
	For i = 1 to STOK68T.GetRowCount()
		if i > 1 Then pe1.PageBreak
		Call YMamulTanitimKartiYazdir(pe1, i)
	Next

	pe1.Show

End Sub
Sub YMamulTanitimKartiYazdir(pe1, satirNo)
	logo = Doc.Getd7Directory_DataFirm & trim(Doc.Customp_Gets("!LOGO"))
'	logo = Doc.Getd7Directory_DataFirm & "images\logo2.bmp"
	res = pe1.PenSize(0.3)
	res = pe1.Box(UstMargin, solMargin, AltMargin - UstMargin, sagMargin - solMargin)
	res = pe1.Line(UstMargin + 11, solMargin, 0, sagMargin - solMargin)
	res = pe1.NoPen()
	res = pe1.Picture(UstMargin + 0.5, solmargin + 1, 10,30, logo, 114)
	res = pe1.PenSize(0.3)

	res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 12)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(UstMargin + 0.5, solMargin + 35, 15, sagMargin - solMargin - 40, "HAMMADDE TANITIM KARTI")
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")

	Gen1 = 17
	Aralik = 5
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 9)
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.Write(UstMargin + 12, solMargin + 1, 5, Gen1, "Kod")
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow", 12)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 5, sagMargin - pe1.GetRight - 2, ": " & STOK68T.KOD(satirNo))

	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 9)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + Aralik, solMargin + 1, 5, Gen1, "Taným")
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow", 12)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 5, sagMargin - pe1.GetRight - 2, ": " & Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", STOK68T.KOD(satirNo), ""))

	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 9)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + Aralik, solMargin + 1, 5, Gen1, "Tedarikçi")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 5, sagMargin - pe1.GetRight - 2, ": " & Doc.GetDBFieldValue("CARI00", "AD", STOK68E.CHCODE, ""))

	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + Aralik, solMargin + 1, 5, Gen1, "Giriþ Tarihi")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 5, sagMargin - pe1.GetRight - 2, ": " & Doc.StrToNorDate(STOK68E.TARIH))

	YaziAlt = pe1.GetBottom()

	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + Aralik, solMargin + 1, 5, Gen1, "Lot No")
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 12)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 6, 30, ": " & STOK68T.LOTNUMBER(satirNo))

	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 6, 14, "Miktar")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 6, sagMargin - pe1.GetRight - 2, ": " & Doc.NumFormat(STOK68T.SF_MIKTAR(satirNo),18,Doc.SigDig(STOK68T.SF_MIKTAR(satirNo))) & " " & trim(STOK68T.SF_SF_UNIT(satirNo)))

	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 9)
	res = pe1.NoPen()
	res = pe1.Barcode(pe1.GetTop + 10, solMargin + 3, 10, 50, "CODE39", trim(STOK68T.LOTNUMBER(satirNo)), "")

	res = pe1.PenSize(0.3)
	res = pe1.Line(YaziAlt + 7, solMargin + 58, 0, sagMargin - solMargin - 58)
	Ust = pe1.GetTop
	sol = pe1.GetLeft
	res = pe1.Line(pe1.GetTop + 4, pe1.GetLeft, 0, sagMargin - pe1.GetLeft)
	res = pe1.Line(Ust, sol, AltMargin - Ust, 0)

	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 9)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
	res = pe1.Write(Ust + 0.3, sol, 5, sagMargin - sol, "KALÝTE KONTROL")
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
End Sub                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         


----------------------------------------------------------------
Form Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : AK2010 / Uygulama : PFRM7S
Makro Kodu : LOADLIST
Hangi Tablo için : STOK19
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : 
Açýklama : Yükleme Listesi
Kod : 
Public 	sayfaBoyu, sayfaEni, UstMargin, SolMargin, AltMargin, sagMargin, S19T, Grid, satirBaslangic, siraNo
Public SayfaSatirSayisi, satirYuksekligi
Sub PrintForm()
	sayfaBoyu	= 210
	sayfaEni = 297
	UstMargin = 10
	SolMargin = 10
	AltMargin = sayfaBoyu - 10
	sagMargin = sayfaEni - 10
	SayfaSatirSayisi = 35
	satirYuksekligi = 3.6

	siraNo = 0

	Set pe1 = doc.CreatePrintEngineOutput()
	res = pe1.Open(0,"Load List" & trim(STOK19E.EVRAKNO), sayfaEni, sayfaBoyu, "LANDSCAPE")


	STOK19T.SafePack()
	Set S19T = Doc.Select2("se3333", "STOK19T", "", "", -1)
	For i = 1 to S19T.GetRowCount()
		if trim(S19T.PACKNO_1(i)) = "" Then 
			S19T.PACKNO_1(i) = "MAKARA" & i
			S19T.OTHERUNIT(i) = S19T.SF_MIKTAR(i)
		Else	
			UnitLength = Doc.GetUDFValue("STOK00", S19T.KOD(i), "BOY", 0)
			S19T.OTHERUNIT(i) = Doc.Div(S19T.SF_MIKTAR(i), UnitLength)
		End if
		MusteriStokKodu = Doc.GetCHStokKodu(S19T.KOD(i), STOK19E.CHKODU)
		S19T.PACKNO_2(i) = MusteriStokKodu
	Next
	S19T.Sort("PACKNO_2+KOD")
	S19T.GroupBy2("PACKNO_1+ALLOC_SIPARTNO")
	For i = 1 to S19T.GetRowCount()
		par1 = S19T.KOD(i)
		par2 = S19T.SF_MIKTAR(i)
		par3 = "22..34"
		NetAgirlik = Doc.RunMacro3("MAKR7S.CALC_BOM", "Calc_KG2", par1, par2, par3)
		S19T.NETAGIRLIK(i) = Doc.Round(NetAgirlik, 0)
		AmbalajAgirligi = S19T.HACIM(i)
		if AmbalajAgirligi = 0 Then AmbalajAgirligi = 40
		S19T.BRUTAGIRLIK(i) = S19T.NETAGIRLIK(i) + AmbalajAgirligi
	Next
	Call Alanlar()
	
	Call PrintFormSayfaBasi(pe1)
	For i = 1 to S19T.GetRowCount()
		Call PrintFormSayfaSatir(pe1, i)
	Next
	Call GenelToplamSatiriYazdir(pe1)
	Call PrintFormSayfaSonu(pe1, i)

	res = pe1.PageBreak()
	Call HammaddeListesiniYazdir(pe1)

	res = pe1.Show()
	res = doc.ActivateAfterMacro(1)
End Sub
Sub PrintFormSayfaBasi(pe1)
	res = pe1.StoreSet(1)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 16)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)

	logo = Doc.Getd7Directory_DataFirm & Doc.Customp_Gets("!LOGO")
	res = pe1.NoPen()
	res = pe1.Picture(UstMargin, solMargin, 15, 45, logo, 114)

	res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
	res = pe1.Write(UstMargin, solMargin, 8, sagMargin - solMargin, "LOAD LIST")
	res = pe1.Write(UstMargin, solMargin, 8, sagMargin - solMargin, "LOAD LIST")
	res = pe1.Write(UstMargin, sagMargin - 100, 8, 100, Doc.GetUDFValue("STOK19E", STOK19E.EVRAKNO, "CONT_NO", 0))


	res = pe1.UseSet(1)
	res = pe1.Write(UstMargin + 15, solMargin, 5, 30, "Customer Name")
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 5, 70, ": " & STOK19E.CHKODU_CARI00_AD)

	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, sagMargin - 90, 5, 10, "Date")
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 5, 20, ": " & Doc.StrToNorDate(Doc.BuGun))

	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 10, 5, 23, "Load List No")
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 5, 27, ": " & trim(STOK19E.EVRAKNO))

	res = pe1.PenSize(0.3)
	res = pe1.Line(pe1.GetTop + 6, solMargin, 0, sagMargin - solMargin)

	BaslikUst = pe1.GetTop + 1
	res = pe1.setalign("CENTER")
	res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 8)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	Sol = SolMargin + 1
	For i = 0 To UBound(Grid,1)
		SutunGenisligi = Grid(i,1)
		Baslik = Grid(i,2)
		res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
		if Grid(i,4) = "N" Then res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
		If SutunGenisligi > 0 Then	
			res = pe1.write(BaslikUst, Sol, 8, SutunGenisligi - 2 , Baslik)
			Sol = Sol + SutunGenisligi
		End If
	Next

	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.Line(pe1.GetBottom, solMargin, 0, sagMargin - solMargin)
	satirBaslangic = pe1.GetBottom() + 3
End Sub
Sub PrintFormSayfaSatir(pe1, satirNo)
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 8)

	if siraNo >= sayfaSatirSayisi Then
		res = pe1.PageBreak()
		Call PrintFormSayfaBasi(pe1)
		siraNo = 0
	End if

	siraNo = siraNo + 1
	satirPozr = satirBaslangic + (siraNo - 1) * satirYuksekligi

	res = pe1.StoreSet(1)
	if siraNo = 1 Then
		res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 11)
		res = pe1.Write(satirPozr, solMargin + 1, 6, 25, "Unit Type")
		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
		If InStr(1, S19T.PACKNO_1(satirNo), "MAKARA") > 0 Then
			res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 6, 50, "DRUM")
		Else
			res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 6, 50, "PALLET")
		End if
		res = pe1.UseSet(1)
		siraNo = siraNo + 2
		satirPozr = satirBaslangic + (siraNo - 1) * satirYuksekligi
	End if

'	res = pe1.setbold(0)
	Sol = SolMargin + 1
	res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")
	StokKodu = S19T.KOD(satirNo)
	MusteriStokKodu = Doc.GetCHStokKodu(StokKodu, STOK19E.CHKODU)
	MusteriSipiNo = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK40E", "CHSIPNO", S19T.ALLOC_SIPNO(satirNo), "")
	Qty = 1
'	If InStr(1, S19T.PACKNO_1(satirNo), "MAKARA") > 0 Then
	If trim(S19T.PACKNO_1(satirNo)) = "" Then
		UnitLength = S19T.SF_MIKTAR(satirNo)
	Else
		UnitLength = Doc.GetUDFValue("STOK00", StokKodu, "BOY", 0)
	End if
	UQty = Doc.Div(S19T.SF_MIKTAR(satirNo), UnitLength)
	Pack = ""

	For i=0 To UBound(Grid,1)
		SutunGenisligi = Grid(i,1)
		DegiskenAdi = Grid(i,0)
		If Left(DegiskenAdi,1) <> "_" Then
			Degisken = S19T.GetFieldValue(DegiskenAdi,SatirNo)
		Else
			If DegiskenAdi = "_MKOD" Then
				Degisken = MusteriStokKodu
				if trim(Degisken) = "" Then Degisken = StokKodu
			ElseIf DegiskenAdi = "_ORDERNO" Then
				Degisken = MusteriSipiNo
			ElseIf DegiskenAdi = "_QTY" Then
				Degisken = Qty
			ElseIf DegiskenAdi = "_UQTY" Then
				Degisken = UQty
			ElseIf DegiskenAdi = "_PCK" Then
				Degisken = Pack
			ElseIf DegiskenAdi = "_ULENGTH" Then
				Degisken = UnitLength
			End If
		End If
		res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
		If Grid(i,4) = "N" Then
			res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
			Ondalik = Grid(i,6)
			Degisken = Doc.NumFormat(Degisken, 20,Ondalik)
		ElseIf Grid(i,4) = "D" Then
			Degisken = Doc.StrtoNordate(Degisken)
		End If
		res = pe1.write(satirpozr, Sol, satiryuksekligi, SutunGenisligi - 2, Degisken)
		Sol = Sol + SutunGenisligi
	Next					 

	if satirNo < S19T.GetRowCount() Then
		if S19T.KOD(satirNo) <> S19T.KOD(satirNo + 1) Then
			Call ToplamSatiriYazdir(pe1, satirNo)
			res = pe1.UseSet(1)
		End if
	Else
		Call ToplamSatiriYazdir(pe1, satirNo)
		res = pe1.UseSet(1)
	End if
End Sub
Sub ToplamSatiriYazdir(pe1, satirNo)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 8)

	siraNo = siraNo + 1
	satirPozr = satirBaslangic + (siraNo - 1) * satirYuksekligi

	Sol = SolMargin + 1
	res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")
	Set S19T2 = Doc.Select2("STOK19_GR", "se3333", "KOD='" & S19T.KOD(satirNo) & "'", "", -1)
	Qty = S19T2.GetRowCount()

	S19T2.GroupBy("KOD")

	If InStr(1, S19T.PACKNO_1(satirNo), "MAKARA") > 0 Then
		UnitLength = S19T2.SF_MIKTAR
	Else
		UnitLength = Doc.GetUDFValue("STOK00", S19T.KOD(satirNo), "BOY", 0)
	End if
	UQty = Doc.Div(S19T2.SF_MIKTAR, UnitLength)
	Pack = ""

	For i=0 To UBound(Grid,1)
		SutunGenisligi = Grid(i,1)
		DegiskenAdi = Grid(i,0)
		If Left(DegiskenAdi,1) <> "_" Then
			if DegiskenAdi = "KOD" Then
				Degisken = ""
			Elseif DegiskenAdi = "KOD_STOK00_AD" Then
				Degisken = "Total"
			Elseif DegiskenAdi = "PACKNO_1" or DegiskenAdi = "ALLOC_SIPNO" Then
				Degisken = ""
			Else
				Degisken = S19T2.GetFieldValue(DegiskenAdi,1)
			End if
		Else
			If DegiskenAdi = "_MKOD" Then
				Degisken = MusteriStokKodu
			ElseIf DegiskenAdi = "_ORDERNO" Then
				Degisken = MusteriSipiNo
			ElseIf DegiskenAdi = "_QTY" Then
				Degisken = Qty
			ElseIf DegiskenAdi = "_UQTY" Then
				Degisken = UQty
			ElseIf DegiskenAdi = "_PCK" Then
				Degisken = Pack
			ElseIf DegiskenAdi = "_ULENGTH" Then
				Degisken = 0
			End If
		End If
		res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
		If Grid(i,4) = "N" Then
			res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
			Ondalik = Grid(i,6)
			Degisken = Doc.NumFormat(Degisken, 20,Ondalik)
		ElseIf Grid(i,4) = "D" Then
			Degisken = Doc.StrtoNordate(Degisken)
		End If
		res = pe1.write(satirpozr, Sol, satiryuksekligi, SutunGenisligi - 2, Degisken)
		Sol = Sol + SutunGenisligi
	Next					 

	siraNo = siraNo + 1
End Sub
Sub GenelToplamSatiriYazdir(pe1)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 8)

	siraNo = siraNo + 1
	satirPozr = satirBaslangic + (siraNo - 1) * satirYuksekligi

	Sol = SolMargin + 1
	res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")
	Set S19T2 = Doc.Select2("STOK19_GR", "se3333", "", "", -1)
	Qty = S19T2.GetRowCount()

	S19T2.GroupBy("EVRAKNO")

	Pack = ""

	For i=0 To UBound(Grid,1)
		SutunGenisligi = Grid(i,1)
		DegiskenAdi = Grid(i,0)
		If Left(DegiskenAdi,1) <> "_" Then
			if DegiskenAdi = "KOD" Then
				Degisken = ""
			Elseif DegiskenAdi = "KOD_STOK00_AD" Then
				Degisken = "Total By Unit Type"
			Elseif DegiskenAdi = "PACKNO_1" or DegiskenAdi = "ALLOC_SIPNO" Then
				Degisken = ""
			Else
				Degisken = S19T2.GetFieldValue(DegiskenAdi,1)
			End if
		Else
			If DegiskenAdi = "_MKOD" Then
				Degisken = MusteriStokKodu
			ElseIf DegiskenAdi = "_ORDERNO" Then
				Degisken = MusteriSipiNo
			ElseIf DegiskenAdi = "_QTY" Then
				Degisken = Qty
				Call KTA_Yaz("QTY", Qty)
			ElseIf DegiskenAdi = "_UQTY" Then
				Degisken = UQty
			ElseIf DegiskenAdi = "_PCK" Then
				Degisken = Pack
			ElseIf DegiskenAdi = "_ULENGTH" Then
				Degisken = 0
			End If
		End If
		res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
		If Grid(i,4) = "N" Then
			res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
			Ondalik = Grid(i,6)
			Degisken = Doc.NumFormat(Degisken, 20,Ondalik)
		ElseIf Grid(i,4) = "D" Then
			Degisken = Doc.StrtoNordate(Degisken)
		End If
		res = pe1.write(satirpozr, Sol, satiryuksekligi, SutunGenisligi - 2, Degisken)
		Sol = Sol + SutunGenisligi
	Next					 
	Call KTA_Yaz("UQTY", S19T2.OTHERUNIT)
	Call KTA_Yaz("NET_KG", S19T2.NETAGIRLIK)
	Call KTA_Yaz("BRUT_KG", S19T2.BRUTAGIRLIK)
	Call KTA_Yaz("TOPLAMMIK", S19T2.SF_MIKTAR)

	siraNo = siraNo + 1
End Sub


Sub PrintFormSayfaSonu(pe1, i)
End Sub
Sub Alanlar()
	ReDim Grid(11,7)
	Grid(0,0) = "KOD_STOK00_AD"
	Grid(1,0) = "_MKOD"
	Grid(2,0) = "KOD"
	Grid(3,0) = "_ORDERNO"
	Grid(4,0) = "PACKNO_1"
	Grid(5,0) = "_QTY"
	Grid(6,0) = "OTHERUNIT"
	Grid(7,0) = "_ULENGTH"
	Grid(8,0) = "SF_MIKTAR"
	Grid(9,0) = "NETAGIRLIK"
	Grid(10,0) = "BRUTAGIRLIK"
	Grid(11,0) = "ALLOC_SIPNO"

	Grid(0,1) = 57
	Grid(1,1) = 22
	Grid(2,1) = 25
	Grid(3,1) = 22
	Grid(4,1) = 20
	Grid(5,1) = 10
	Grid(6,1) = 15
	Grid(7,1) = 18
	Grid(8,1) = 21	
	Grid(9,1) = 21	
	Grid(10,1) = 21
	Grid(11,1) = 20

	Grid(0,2) = "Product Description"
	Grid(1,2) = "Customer Code"
	Grid(2,2) = "Our Code"
	Grid(3,2) = "Order No"
	Grid(4,2) = "Bulk/Pallet No"
	Grid(5,2) = "Qty"
	Grid(6,2) = "Unit Qty (pcs)"
	Grid(7,2) = "Unit Length (m)"
	Grid(8,2) = "Cable Length (m)"
	Grid(9,2) = "Net Weight (kg)"
	Grid(10,2) = "Gross Weight (kg)"
	Grid(11,2) = "Our Ref."

	Grid(0,4) = "C"
	Grid(1,4) = "C"
	Grid(2,4) = "C"
	Grid(3,4) = "C"
	Grid(4,4) = "C"
	Grid(5,4) = "N"
	Grid(6,4) = "N"
	Grid(7,4) = "N"
	Grid(8,4) = "N"
	Grid(9,4) = "N"
	Grid(10,4) = "N"
	Grid(11,4) = "C"

	For i = 0 To UBound(Grid,1)
		If Grid(i,4)="N" Then
			Degisken = Grid(i,0)
			ondalik = S19T.MaxSigDig(Degisken)	
			if Grid(i,0) = "_ULENGTH" Then Ondalik = 1
			Grid(i,6) = Ondalik			
		End If
	Next	
End Sub
Sub HammaddeListesiniYazdir(pe1)
	Call PrintFormSayfaBasi(pe1)
	Set HmdTable = GetHmdTable()

	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 12)
	res = pe1.Write(satirBaslangic, solMargin, 6, 100, "HAMMADDE TOPLAMLARI")
	For i = 1 to HmdTable.GetRowCount()
		satirPozr = satirBaslangic + i * (satirYuksekligi + 2)
		res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 10)
		res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
		res = pe1.Write(satirPozr, solMargin, 6, 50, HmdTable.ACIKLAMA(i))
		res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
		res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 5, 30, Doc.NumFormat(HmdTable.BOMREC_KAYNAK0(i), 14,2))
		res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
		Birim = HmdTable.ACIKLAMA2(i)
		res = pe1.Write(satirPozr, pe1.GetRight + 1, 6, 20, trim(Birim))
	Next
End Sub
Function GetHmdTable()
	Set bem = Doc.CreateSObject("BATCHERRORMODE")

	Set BomTable = GetBomTable()
	TableStr = BomTable.GetAppName()
	Set HmdTable = Doc.Select2("de3ft5", TableStr, "BOMREC_INPUTTYPE='H'", "", -1)
	For i = 1 to HmdTable.GetRowCount() 
		GK_9 = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "GK_9", HmdTable.BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE(i), "")
		if trim(GK_9) = "" Then
			Doc.d7MsgBox HmdTable.BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE(i) & " Stok Kartý Grup Kodu-9 boþ", "", MB_OK
			HmdTable.ACIKLAMA(i) = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", HmdTable.BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE(i), "")
		Else
			HmdTable.ACIKLAMA(i) = Doc.GetGecoValue("STKGK9", "AD", GK_9, -1)
		End if
		HmdTable.ACIKLAMA2(i) = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "IUNIT", HmdTable.BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE(i), "")
	Next
	HmdTable.GroupBy("ACIKLAMA+ACIKLAMA2")
	Set GetHmdTable = HmdTable
End Function
Function GetBomTable()
	Doc.CreateTableObject "BMR1=WBMRSYS", "TMPCOPY"
	Set GetBomTable = Doc.GetTableObject("BMR1")
	Set S19T1 = Doc.Select2("sd33", "STOK19T", "", "", -1)
	S19T1.GroupBy2("KOD")
	For i = 1 to S19T1.GetRowCount()
		Set BomTable = Calc_Bom2(S19T1.KOD(i), S19T1.SF_MIKTAR(i), "22", "34")
		if BomTable.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
			GetBomTable.AppendTable(BomTable)
		Else
			Doc.d7MsgBox S19T1.KOD(i) & " ürün aðacý bulunamýyor", "", MB_OK
		End if
	Next
End Function
Function Calc_Bom2(StokKodu, Miktar, StopStokKodu1, StopStokKodu2)
	ret = Doc.Calc_Bom("M","M", StokKodu,1,Miktar,"","","","","","","","")  	 	
	Set Tbmrsys = Doc.GetTableObject("_WBMRSYS_SELECT") 		

	For i = 1 to Tbmrsys.GetRowCount()
		if Not ((Tbmrsys.IALTSEVIYEBULUNDU(i) = 0 and _
							(Tbmrsys.BOMREC_CODE(i) < StopStokKodu1 or Tbmrsys.BOMREC_CODE(i) > StopStokKodu2)) or _
			(Tbmrsys.BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE(i) >= StopStokKodu1 and Tbmrsys.BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE(i) <= StopStokKodu2)) Then
			Tbmrsys.BOMREC_INPUTTYPE(i) = ""
			Tbmrsys.BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE(i) = ""
		End if
	Next
	Tbmrsys.SafePack()
	Set Calc_Bom2 = Tbmrsys
End Function
Sub KTA_Yaz(AlanAdi, Deger)
	Doc.UFLD00_CREATEEDITTABLE "T_U0001", "STOK19E", STOK19E.EVRAKNO
	Set T_U1 = Doc.GetTableObject("T_U0001")   
	T_U1.SetFieldValue AlanAdi, -1, Deger
	T_U1.Exec "SAVE"
End Sub

Form Kaynak Kodu
